## Purpose and recovery limits

Sean requested an exhaustive durable original-agent record, not an executive summary. This document combines
the detailed predecessor handoff, all available user/assistant messages and outbound coordination prompts,
recovered incoming coordination, and relevant repository records. It is intentionally long and includes
historical contradictions explicitly as history. No private reasoning, credentials, or inaccessible history
is included. Repository files were read only; this archive is the only deliverable and lives in authorized staging.

The read_thread interface exposed 20 turns including this archive turn; 19 earlier turns are preserved below.
This is all available pagination, not a claim that every original tool byte or hidden event survived.
Incoming messages were retrieved with outputs enabled and a 20,000-character per-item ceiling.
Tool histories may contain truncated results; missing/truncated records are not silently reconstructed.
Original screenshot filenames are preserved, but image pixels are not embedded into this Markdown.
Temporary screenshot paths may no longer exist. Old links retain their historical paths as source evidence;
resolve repository-relative paths against the canonical new root.

The history section preserves publicly visible messages and cross-agent coordination, not private deliberation.
The repository appendix preserves complete selected source-record values in readable key/value form.
These are snapshots, not authorization to restore obsolete mechanics.

## Latest authoritative interpretation and corrections

The successor Level Design 2 (01a14b9d-de3f-74c0-bd72-b5ac632f4bb8) verified current version 0.55.0,
Prototype 51 checkpoint integration, and Prototype 53 forward V-shaped Widow recovery.
Those later records supersede uncertainties and older return-to-origin behavior in my historical handoff.

The current first-level route is 9600 pixels wide, with floor y440, start x85, opening boundary x3120,
and temporary completion x9450. There is exactly one checkpoint at x4650, about49% along the route.
The integrated first level is continuous, not a separate20-second trial. All six kill-count gates were removed.
Twelve opening events and36 later finite runners are potential arrivals, not required kills.
Crowds cap at8, or6 with a Widow; only one Widow is allowed. Skipped/retired enemies are not kills.
All20 raised surfaces use moss-green v3 art; floor and noncolliding recessed supports remain.
No playable Clogfather was delivered by this agent. A temporary exit is not final fulfillment of Sean's boss request.

My last known shooter cap was2. The successor found an existing uncommitted change to3 across
balance/layout/state/tests/data.
I did not own, receive, or establish approval for that change. Preserve it while investigating provenance; do not revert
or bless it based solely on this archive. I received no approval for a second or boss checkpoint after exactly-one.
The90-enemy full-route proposal, pickups, boss1080HP/45–60-second target, portal, and alternative-perspective levels

The sole machine's one selected-hero clone, retained on healthy check-in and consumed on first replacement,

new run resets stock. Three lives remain. My responsibility was location and integrated regression coverage.

Current repository AGENTS.md was read during archival. It includes project-specific art, playtest, build,
roadmap, storybook, bestiary, combat metadata and writing-style guidance. Sean also provided replacement global
AGENTS instructions in this conversation. Apply current instructions by precedence; the historical handoff's
discussion of past instruction replacement is not permission to ignore current repository rules.

## Concrete next work and ownership

No unfinished Level Design-owned edit remained after the x4650 handoff.

Art owned production assets/manifests; Mechanics owned balance and Widow behavior; Storyboarding owned narrative.

The successor should reconcile current descriptive documents with the implemented continuous ungated route and
single checkpoint, while retaining historical storybook entries and old concepts as history.
Validate actual platform readability, opening pressure, jump choices, and Widow fairness with both heroes;
headless invulnerable bot traversal is not human pacing or survival evidence.
The broader level still needs a playable Clogfather, appropriate boss retry/completion narrative,
and approved expansion of mixed encounters, entrances and pickups if Sean wants those proposals developed.
Do not add a second checkpoint, portal,90-enemy quota, or a boss HP value merely because an old concept mentions it.
Check the unresolved shooter-cap edit with the responsible owner before making balance changes.

All next implementation requires fresh ownership coordination in the successor chats and current repository inspection.
Do not launch or foreground the game from this archive task. No production code was changed during archival.

### User request 1

You are the Level Design agent. Your job will be to help design each level of the game including, but not limited to:

- Assigning platform positions
- Communicating with the Art agent to get create backgrounds and foreground debris that the heroes can jump onto so
  they're not always jumping onto floating flat platforms (which would be very boring)

- Determining which monsters are present, how many, where they are positioned, etc
- How long each level is horizontally and chronologically
- Possibly creating whole new types of level that are not strictly 2D side-scrolling (for example: Contra 3: The Alien
  Wars had some levels that were overhead. Maybe we can design a few levels that are not 2D as well (they don't have to
  be overhead like Contra 3's))

- Anything else related to the creation of levels

Your first task is to talk to the Storyboard agent and Engineering in order to convert the first level of the game,
which used to be a tutorial, into a full-length playable level with numerous monsters, platforms, foreground objects to
jump on/over, etc.

Feel free to do some research into other 2D Contra (and clone) games if you are not already a level design expert. Don't
copy anything directly but feel free to learn from other game designs.

## My request:

Take a look at some screens from Contra 3: The Alien Wars for SNES.

In the first 20 seconds of the game, the player is inundated by numerous enemies that come in from the left side of the
screen, the right, weapon powerups flying above, robot things coming up from the ground, enemies pitchingn projectiles.
It's very busy/noisy.

I want our game to be like that. Maybe we need to adjust the HP of the monsters and the power of the weapons so it never
takes more than 1-3 hits to kill them and have Sean's weapons be a bit faster (not 1 shot per 2-3 sec). Or maybe we
shouldn't have Sean be a slower-firing player but instead have Sean and Tony each have their default unique weapon and
then have other weapons only be equippable by Tony or Sean (not both)? I'm not sure. Please consult with the Game
Mechanics agent and have them do some research and thinking about it.

Also notice how there are more complex platforms in the level (in one of the screenshots). There is a bad guy shooting
at the player from atop one. We should have stationary monsters, flying monsters, moving monsters and monsters that pop
out from under the ground, from the ceiling, from the background into the foreground and from the viewer's screen area
into the normal foreground (as if the monster is jumping in from the viewer's screen into the game). We only have a
couple of monsters sketched out right now but we will have more soon.

Either way, the first level needs a lot of work. It also doesn't have the Clogfather at all in it, which it should at
the end.

Let's come up with a plan and some mockups of the new platforming, ideas for how many additional monsters to have,
adjustments to weapon power and monster HP so we can have the game be busier and more action-packed. Have engineering
present the report.

## Role, collaborators, and ownership

Sean assigned this chat Level Design: platform placement, level length and pacing, enemy placement/counts, environmental
objects players can climb, coordination with Storyboarding and Art, and eventually alternative level formats.

The immediate objective was to turn the former tutorial into a full first level with dense action, meaningful
platforming, varied enemy entrances, and the Clogfather at the end. Sean explicitly requested coordination with Game
Mechanics and asked Engineering to present the combined report.

Existing collaborators:

| Role | Chat ID |
|---|---|
| Engineering | `01a1375f-7657-7580-b16e-57a6f5925efd` |
| Game Mechanics | `01a138a7-cc43-7420-a950-55b935ddde25` |
| Bestiary / Art | `01a1388b-2b60-7011-832a-5c6a044e0028` |
| Storyboarding | `01a1387e-545b-7671-943e-3d36a1c88db7` |

Engineering owned integration, base game changes, builds, versions, roadmap, storybook, and the final user report. My
edits were assigned subsets, coordinated to avoid shared-file collisions. Mechanics owned weapon balance and later the
Widow behavior. Art owned artwork and asset manifests.

The project was relocated to `/Users/sfoley/repos/drain_damage`. Do not recreate `/Users/sfoley/repos/plumbing_inc`.

## User intent and corrections

The original Contra screenshots illustrated the intended feel:

- Many enemies arriving from both horizontal sides.
- Flying threats and powerups.
- Stationary elevated shooters and projectile throwers.
- Ground, ceiling, background-to-foreground, and viewer-to-play-plane entrances.
- Architecture and debris supporting platforms, rather than repeated floating slabs.
- Ordinary enemies generally dying in one to three hits.
- Sean’s weapon firing much faster than the perceived two-to-three-second interval.
- Distinct weapon identities for Tony and Sean, with potentially character-exclusive additional weapons.
- A full level culminating in the Clogfather.

Those were direction and requests for design consultation, not approval of every possible new enemy or entrance
mechanic.

Subsequent explicit corrections, relayed through Mechanics/Engineering and implemented within my assigned scope:

1. **Remove every first-level kill-count progress gate.** Replace gated encounters with larger crowds and finite waves
   along a freely traversable route.

2. **Make the flying spider twice its linear size and durable.** This became a deliberate exception to ordinary enemies’
   one-to-three-hit target.

3. **Give the Widow an actual proximity swoop attack matching its magazine description.** A passive fixed hover was
   superseded.

4. **Audit contrast across every first-level platform.** The initial pale/ivory treatment did not cover the large
   machinery. The later direction was dank moss/gray-green frames and readable treads, dark undercuts, no beige or glow.

5. **Exactly one checkpoint near halfway through the level.** This superseded three checkpoint machines. One sleeping
   clone should occupy the sole machine; after dispensing, it should be empty.

6. Preserve three lives, scenery, and free traversal.
7. During the daughter’s playtest, do not foreground or launch the game. All my checks remained headless.

## Last delivered level structure

The active first-level implementation was:

`integrated_first_call.gd` → `opening_trial_game.gd` → `game.gd`

Engineering switched normal Start to the integrated class. The old trial class remained as an internal
implementation/test dependency; it was no longer intended as the normal separate timed experience.

The integrated level:

- World width: **9,600 pixels**.
- Temporary completion threshold: **x = 9,450**.
- Player starting x: **85**.
- Opening replacement ends at **x = 3,120**.
- The opening uses the first three platform modules from an earlier 6,000-pixel trial layout.
- Remaining authored scenery stays in its original world coordinates.
- The opening continues beyond 20 seconds; no timed-results stop.
- Checkpoint/respawn does not recreate opening enemies or reset world progress.
- The final delivered checkpoint constant was **`CHECKPOINTS := [4650.0]`**.

### Why x4650

The midpoint between spawn85 and exit9450 is4767.5. The existing cabinet rendering would overlap the next platform
beginning at4850 if placed at that exact midpoint.

At checkpoint4650:

- Progress is approximately49% of the playable route.
- The floor is clear between the previous obstacle ending4000 and the next beginning4850.
- Cabinet visible bounds were approximately4643.5–4776.4.
- This leaves approximately73.6 pixels before the next platform.
- No scenery or collision layout needed moving.

The existing integrated loops derive machine placement, activation triggers, and enemy-spawn exclusions from
`Level.CHECKPOINTS`; changing the constant removed the other placements automatically.

## Ungated encounter implementation

I delivered this in `scripts/integrated_first_call.gd` and updated `tests/integrated_opening.gd`.

### Opening

Twelve finite opening event positions:

`100, 250, 450, 600, 800, 1000, 1200, 1400, 1650, 1900, 2200, 2450`

Their eligibility times were set to zero in the integrated copy of the layout. Position determines when they become
eligible; safety and population checks can defer them.

Passing the opening marks remaining unissued opening events unavailable. It does not award kills. Existing live actors
persist until normal retirement/death.

### Later waves

| Range | Maximum arrivals |
|---|---:|
| x3500–4800 | 8 |
| x4900–6480 | 10 |
| x6860–8160 | 10 |
| x8400–9380 | 8 |

Total: **36 later runners**, up from the old22 encounter quota, plus the opening’s12 potential events.

Important distinctions:

- These are finite available arrivals, not a guarantee the player must fight all48 enemies.
- Moving past a wave’s range bypasses its unissued remainder.
- No kill requirement blocks forward travel or the temporary exit.
- Arrivals are staggered by at least0.65 seconds.
- At most one arrival is created per encounter update.
- Deferred arrivals do not accumulate into a burst.
- Front/rear entrance selection prefers rear every third arrival and tries the alternate side if needed.
- Entrances are offscreen.
- Player clearance is measured from the expanded visible Flushfiend bounds, not merely its anchor.
- Required clearance:220 pixels.
- Spawn placement avoids scenery, other bodies, opening actors, and checkpoint proximity.
- Checkpoint exclusion: within200 pixels of its location.
- Enemies sufficiently far behind the camera retire without kill credit.
- Retirement threshold used `enemy.pos.x + 38 < camera_x - 320`.
- No live threats are deleted merely to satisfy a population cap.

### Population budget

- Eight bodies normally.
- Six while a live or warning/entering Widow exists.
- One Widow at a time.
- A new Widow is deferred when population is already six.
- Population conservatively counts campaign enemies, live opening actors, and live swarm actors.

Mechanics also wanted two shooters, six hostile bullets, and three independent attack patterns. Opening state already
managed those attack budgets. The later waves I added were runners, not additional shooters.

### Gates and completion

All six former shutters were removed from the integrated level’s:

- Collision.
- Projectile blocking.
- Drawing.
- Kill-count progression.

Legacy mission arrays/indices remained for compatibility/telemetry:

- `mission.zone_counts.fill(0)`
- `mission.current_zone = 2`
- `gate_progress.fill(1)`

The integrated movement and projectile-solid methods use the replacement level geometry, not the legacy gate geometry.

HUD changed to following the service route rather than an enemy quota.

**The Clogfather was still missing at my last verified handoff.** Completion at9450 was explicitly temporary, and
Engineering was told not to describe the boss milestone as complete.

## Combat and Widow details

|---|---:|---:|

| Sean | 18 | 0.24 seconds |

Later runners use24HP, giving two hits from either default weapon.

The Widow:

- Twice its original linear render size.
- Collision expanded consistently to60×48.
- 180HP.

### Superseded hover behavior

Mechanics first delivered a proximity-triggered fixed four-second hover, then resumed leftward travel. This was
explicitly replaced after Sean requested a real swoop matching the magazine.

### Latest swoop handoff received

Mechanics reported:

-0.65-second visible warning.

- Aim locked at warning start.

-0.8-second committed dive.

- No tracking after commitment.
- Expanded full-body sweep against `player_shot_solids`, including floor.
- Blocked dive aborts into a one-second return.

-1.2-second cooldown.
-2× scale and180HP preserved.

- Tests cover open-floor dive, warning, fixed aim, recovery, and deck nonpenetration.

I did not edit the parent adapter during that work; Mechanics owned it.

### Clearance constraints I supplied

Opening decks are250×16 at y320. With flyer center/base lane250 and a24-pixel shallow arc, its doubled bounds originally
stayed above the deck.

For the attack:

- Gaps between plinths and decks are only40 or50 pixels.
- A60-pixel-wide flyer cannot safely pass through them.
- The under-deck opening is104 pixels high, from336 to440.
- A48-pixel flyer plus46-pixel hero nearly fills that space.
- Do not trap the hero under a deck with the large body.
- Sweep against real solids, abort blocked descent, and preserve an escape direction.
- Only the actual play-plane body should deal contact damage.

## Other implementation details worth preserving

### Shared projectile update

The integrated class had to avoid advancing friendly bullets twice through parent/base combat:

1. Save friendly shots and temporarily give base combat an empty list while it moves campaign enemies.
2. Restore shots.
3. Run one integrated closest-hit sweep across scenery, opening actors, campaign enemies, and swarm.
4. Temporarily hide friendly shots again when calling the parent’s hostile-shot update.

Do not casually replace this with both parent updates; it previously required explicit single-travel regression
coverage.

### Flushfiend collision and death audio

- Visible projectile hit body is43 pixels tall.
- Helper returns `Rect2(feet + Vector2(-19,-43), Vector2(38,43))`.
- Movement/contact body remains36 pixels tall.
- Opening runner/rear-runner hit handling uses the visible projectile body.
- On lethal opening runner/rear-runner damage, call `flushfiend_death_audio.play_defeat()`.
- Do not use that audio for shooter, emerger, or flyer.
- Engineering owns base campaign Flushfiend audio hooks.

### Swarm

- The optional swarm at2090 was removed from the replaced opening.
- The later swarm at4400 was retained.
- Do not conflate it with the Hallowed Widow.
- Its behavior was not otherwise redesigned by my work.
- Base/swarm geometry hooks were adjusted by Engineering to respect the integrated geometry.

### Checkpoints and reset

`preserving_respawn` prevents respawn from resetting the opening observation/world state. Full restart resets it.

Respawn clears hostile-shot state/tokens, preserves progress and weapon, and respects normal life handling.

### Wastewater work recovered from compaction

- Configures from `Data.bundle.wastewater`.

- Stable profile art uses16-pixel strips and mirrored480-pixel tiles.
- Below-floor region approximately456–528.
- Terminal area at8570 plugged.
- Trial-specific cap at1440.
- Engineering integrated it around build44.
- Relevant tests passed then.

No pending wastewater change was assigned at my last handoff.

## Reconciling stale gates, three checkpoints, and timed-trial records

Use this authority order:

1. Sean’s latest direct instructions and corrections.
2. Current integrated runtime and its passing tests.
3. Engineering’s latest verified build/delivery records.
4. Current design documents updated to match those.
5. Historical documents and old implementation classes as historical context.

- **Gates:** no kill-count gates in the integrated first level. Legacy `GATES`, mission classes, gate tests, and base
  implementation may still exist. Their existence does not make them current integrated gameplay.

- **Checkpoints:** one at4650 supersedes3300/6660/8190.
- **Timed trial:** continuous normal first-level opening supersedes the separate20-second experiment. Trial filenames
  and parent classes remain implementation history, not proof of a timed mode in normal Start.

- **Platform palette:** moss-green v3 supersedes ivory v2 for all20 raised surfaces.
- **Widow behavior:** committed swoop supersedes passive four-second hover.
- **Boss:** temporary x9450 exit must remain labeled incomplete until the Clogfather actually exists and controls
  completion.

Preserve historical storybook iterations and source artwork. Update current roadmap/design claims without rewriting
history as though earlier iterations never existed.

updates happened. Your observation that current records still describe obsolete behavior is a real reconciliation task,
not evidence that Sean reversed these corrections.

Earlier project instructions included Art ownership, playtest permission, build notifications, roadmap/storybook
maintenance, character naming, and bestiary/combat metadata rules.

Later Sean supplied an AGENTS.md replacement saying it **replaces all previously provided AGENTS.md instructions**,
retaining global coding conventions. Therefore do not mechanically treat the earlier pasted project instructions as
still authoritative solely because they appear in this history. Read the current repository guidance and honor direct
user instructions and active coordination commitments.

The explicit daughter-playtest restriction and assigned file ownership were separate concrete coordination instructions
during those tasks.

86.49

60

movement_contract.required_horizontal_gap_max:

80

120

movement_contract.continuous_floor:

true

movement_contract.rule:

Validate with slower Sean including acceleration and early release. No lethal pits. Upper paths are optional; grounded
objects may require a single reachable jump.

movement_contract.collision:

Grounded machinery and pipe bundles have solid sides and tops matching their silhouette. Suspended service grates may be
one-way. Do not draw a solid object with walk-through collision.

encounter_contract.playable_species[0]:

toilet

encounter_contract.total_authored_enemies:

30

encounter_contract.maximum_active_attackers:

4

encounter_contract.ordinary_hp_source:

bestiary/flushfiend/monster.json

encounter_contract.hp_override:

null

encounter_contract.activation:

Prefer visible forward spawns with at least 220 pixels of separation. At a closed encounter gate, a queued reinforcement
may arrive to either side only through the telegraphed reinforcement rule. Count telegraphing plus active enemies
against the cap of four.

encounter_contract.pursuit:

Retain approved persistent pursuit after aggro; never silently leash or delete survivors. Delay later waves if earlier
pursuers fill the active cap.

encounter_contract.completion:

A clear objective must account for all 30 authored enemies, including queued enemies. No exit softlock from unspawned
enemies. Finale only begins once earlier encounters are resolved.

encounter_contract.routing:

Enemies cannot pass through solid props. Ground enemies must negotiate low obstacles or have connected alternate routes;
test pursuit at every prop.

encounter_contract.fairness:

No unannounced behind-player spawns, checkpoint spawns, landing-zone spawns or spawns during dialogue. A visible rear
reinforcement is permitted only with the warning, clearance and cancellation rules below.

encounter_contract.gates.decision:

Approve visible finite encounter gates, with a necessary explicit exception for safely telegraphed rear reinforcements.
A gate alone does not guarantee room ahead of a player pressed against it.

encounter_contract.gates.placement:

One gate at the exit of each authored zone, including the final exit. Engineering chooses coordinates after carving out
a clear combat pocket; preserve checkpoint footprints on the far side.

encounter_contract.gates.appearance:

Art supplies a reusable industrial service shutter with closed/open states. Closed silhouette must span the traversable
height and match collision; no invisible walls or code-drawn substitutes.

encounter_contract.gates.behavior:

Starts visibly closed before entry; never drops onto player or enemy. Opens immediately when the zone finite queue and
surviving enemies are cleared; remains open across deaths. No timer padding. Backtracking remains open.

encounter_contract.gates.reinforcements:

Prefer ahead of the hero. If no forward location exists, choose a fully visible clear floor location behind with at
least 220 pixels from player collision bounds, outside checkpoint footprints and predicted jump landing. Show the
existing enemy sprite as a non-colliding flashing warning for at least 1.25 seconds before activation; this reuses art,
not a substitute drawing. No damage or attacks during warning.

encounter_contract.gates.recheck:

Before activation recheck separation, visible camera bounds, prop/enemy overlap and landing safety. If unsafe, cancel
and requeue without consuming a kill or spawn count. Do not pause the whole game; a queued enemy never counts as
defeated.

encounter_contract.gates.liveness:

Reserve at least a 640-pixel continuous clear combat lane before each gate, with two candidate reinforcement locations.
Test rushing gate, leftmost/rightmost player positions, camping candidate locations, repeated jumping, retreat and
checkpoint retry. If no safe candidate exists, reposition terrain or gate; do not reduce warning distance or violate
cap.

encounter_contract.gates.ui:

Show finite zone enemies remaining including queued enemies using existing HUD text; a closed gate must communicate why
it is closed.

encounter_contract.gates.art_dependency:

Engineering supplies exact source dimensions and anchor/collider contract to Bestiary / Art before generation.

zones[5].finale:

First integration uses two finite Flushfiend waves (3 then 2). The Clogfather replaces this fallback only when boss
mechanics, art and timing are validated; never present fallback as boss delivery.

terrain_coordinates:

Each tuple is x, top_y, width, height in logical pixels; these are intended solid object bounds. All bases reach
floor_y=440. Engineering may use a narrower verified art-aligned collider, preserving reachable top surfaces.

spawn_adjustment:

Authored enemy_x values express encounter intent. Do not use nearest-clear-point relocation as final placement: it
clusters homes and reverses intended order. Engineering must reserve broad combat pockets, relocate terrain and
distribute staged homes in route order, outside props and checkpoint footprints.

recovery.checkpoint_x[0]:

3300

recovery.checkpoint_x[1]:

6660

recovery.checkpoint_x[2]:

8190

recovery.clear_radius_pixels:

160

recovery.activation:

Restore to selected hero max health once at each newly reached checkpoint; show plumber tape using existing art.

recovery.death:

Restore health and return to latest checkpoint; cleared enemies remain cleared, queued waves persist. Preserve pursuit
identity but ensure a safe respawn position and grace before contact.

recovery.restart:

Reset all enemies, waves, checkpoints and mission progress. Preserve selected hero and existing story replay rules.

art_contract.owner:

Bestiary / Art

art_contract.engineering_confirms_dimensions:

true

art_contract.suggested_logical_prop_sizes[0][0]:

160

art_contract.suggested_logical_prop_sizes[0][1]:

60

art_contract.suggested_logical_prop_sizes[1][0]:

180

art_contract.suggested_logical_prop_sizes[1][1]:

120

art_contract.suggested_logical_prop_sizes[2][0]:

200

art_contract.suggested_logical_prop_sizes[2][1]:

180

art_contract.background:

Reuse existing basement pending new art; request entry/storage, pressure/pump and sump variants, built for 960x540
viewport and seamless scrolling.

art_contract.foreground:

Transparent PNG props with explicit foot anchors, top-surface coordinates and collision bounds. Decorative debris must
not hide enemies, projectiles or landing edges.

art_contract.priority:

Grounded low pipe bundle, medium pump housing, tall supported assembly, then zone backgrounds and non-colliding
foreground debris.

art_contract.restriction:

No independently generated or code-drawn substitute scenery. Existing art may be reused with its actual collision
semantics; missing pieces remain explicitly pending.

deferred[0]:

Hallowed Widow and other species until playable AI and animations validate

deferred[1]:

Clogfather boss subject to existing combat-design.json, starter range and timing validation

deferred[2]:

Acid drips until telegraph art, safe-lane logic and contact-hit damage scale are validated

deferred[3]:

Portal and destination until Storyboarding resolves story direction

deferred[4]:

Alternative camera/perspective levels: future design exploration, no conversion of this first mission

acceptance[0]:

Both Tony and Sean can traverse every mandatory jump and finish all authored encounters

acceptance[1]:

All 30 enemies are accounted for; at most four active attackers; no spawn or patrol inside a prop

acceptance[2]:

Checkpoints do not activate repeatedly; death, restart and finale wave transitions cannot softlock

acceptance[3]:

Persistent pursuers negotiate props; projectiles use the same solid-world bounds as movement

acceptance[4]:

Camera, floor, background tiling, exit and HUD cover all 9600 pixels without old 2400 limits

acceptance[5]:

Record clear time and damage per zone for both heroes; do not claim target duration as measured

acceptance[6]:

Preserve prior build and capture actual running-game screenshots under the project permission rule

acceptance[7]:

Roadmap and storybook distinguish integrated fallback, pending art and planned boss

acceptance[8]:

Gate liveness and warning-spawn safety pass for both heroes, including rushing and camping; gates never conceal
unresolvable queued enemies.

coordination.story:

design/first-level-narrative.md

coordination.engineering:

handoffs/engineering-level-one-baseline.json

coordination.alignment:

Corporate-HQ service infrastructure and sump finale match Storyboarding. Existing ending applies only to cleared
ordinary enemies; boss and portal need a dedicated approved replacement ending.

coordination.delivery_boundary:

Expanded playable route is an intermediate delivery toward the complete first mission. Do not mark the intended
Clogfather mission complete with a Flushfiend fallback.

coordination.engineering_budget:

Engineering suggested 18–24 enemies over 6000–8000 pixels. Level Design proposes 30 over 9600 with a four-attacker cap;
reduce empty distance or repetitive encounters after measured playtests, not by increasing HP.

coordination.art:

assets/world/level-one-kit-proposal.json

coordination.story_review:

Storyboarding reviewed the six-zone route: no narrative conflicts. All authored enemies must be defeated for current
completion text; fallback must not imply boss defeat, repaired plumbing or headquarters safety.

movement.caveat:

Analytic estimates, not a collision simulation. Discrete physics, acceleration and edge overlap alter reach. Design for
slower Sean and validate both routes.

movement.recommended_geometry:

Required rises 45-65px; comfortable same-height gaps <=100-120px; landing surfaces >=100px. No mandatory max-range
jumps. Higher scenery needs stepping surfaces.

architecture.runtime:

scripts/game.gd owns procedural Node2D movement, collision, combat, camera, checkpoints and victory; no
tilemap/navigation system.

architecture.platform_collision:

One-way tops for player; no side/ceiling collision. Platforms block projectile sweeps. Flat floor always catches player:
no pits/fall deaths.

680

architecture.spawn_x[1]:

394

architecture.completion:

player.x >=2300 and enemies empty; HUD denominator hardcoded 3. Must replace with authored encounter totals and explicit
exit condition.

architecture.camera:

Horizontal only, target player.x-320, speed400; world bounds 0..width-960.

architecture.presentation:

scripts/world_art.gd draws background and sprites independently from collision. New scenery/platform art goes to
Bestiary / Art.

architecture.suggested_refactor:

One validated level-data definition for world bounds, solid/surface rectangles, prop art placement, enemy spawns,
checkpoints and exit. Keep art top surfaces aligned with collision.

enemies.playable:

Flushfiend/toilet only; 40HP, floor-bound sine bounce, contact damage; persistent aggro within horizontal280, patrol80,
chase115, stop6.

enemies.constraints:

No platform traversal, gravity/pathfinding, height-aware detection, projectile attacks or real flying enemy AI. All
enemies currently updated off-camera; persistent pursuit may accumulate a horde. Current movement cannot respect
physical scenery side walls.

enemies.widow:

Damage atlas/80HP metadata available; flight behavior is not implemented. Requires explicit scope and movement
integration; do not claim ready to place as a distinct enemy.

enemies.clogfather:

720HP metadata/HUD preview only; no boss gameplay. Its 30 Sean-hit timing needs retuning before encounter
implementation.

Horizontal muzzle-path range, swept collision; no vertical aiming, melee or explosives implemented.

proposed_encounter_budget.simultaneous_pressure:

Usually 2-3 active threats, brief peaks4; space encounters to prevent unintended persistent-chase stacks.

proposed_encounter_budget.checkpoint_count[0]:

proposed_encounter_budget.checkpoint_count[1]:

3

proposed_encounter_budget.duration_target_minutes[0]:

proposed_encounter_budget.duration_target_minutes[1]:

proposed_encounter_budget.duration_caveat:

Target only. Walking 6000-8000px takes roughly26-34s for Sean; meaningful combat, optional routes and scenery must earn
duration. Do not pad solely with corridor length.

proposed_encounter_budget.first_delivery:

Build an authored route with current toilet enemy; reserve new monster encounters until their behavior is implemented
and verified.

reset_rules:

Death fully heals and teleports to latest checkpoint; defeated enemies remain dead, surviving enemies retain aggro.
Restart recreates encounter and clears checkpoint/aggro. Design safe respawn recovery.

### Source handoffs/level-design-integration-confirmation.json

Absolute source: /Users/sfoley/repos/drain_damage/handoffs/level-design-integration-confirmation.json

SHA-256: bb77fb5cc9cf4385949b6d79097dd4536329ee2a7ddcf5fca6614c68984a97e7

owner:

Level Design

status:

Design decisions resolved; proceed with existing scope. Runtime validation remains Engineering work.

canonical_design:

levels/first-call/level-design.json

story:

design/first-level-narrative.md

art:

assets/world/first-call/manifest.json

superseded_geometry:

handoffs/first-call-runtime-plan.json is an initial audit, not approved final homes. Its status explicitly records
rejection of clustered relocated homes.

design_blockers: empty list.

integration_requirements[0]:

Engineering may relocate props and homes to reserve broad combat lanes, while preserving reachable 60px steps and finite
totals.

integration_requirements[1]:

Six zones, 30 enemies, at most four active plus warning enemies, three checkpoints; visible finite encounter shutters
remain approved.

integration_requirements[2]:

Prefer forward reinforcement. Rear reinforcement allowed only under existing visible warning, separation, cancellation
and recheck rules.

integration_requirements[3]:

Verify shutter cannot be jumped from nearby tall props: closed top is y180, so keep reachable adjacent launch surfaces
sufficiently low or distant. Do not add invisible collision above the visible shutter.

integration_requirements[4]:

### Source design/first-level-narrative.md

Absolute source: /Users/sfoley/repos/drain_damage/design/first-level-narrative.md

SHA-256: 08b0d2fb5031cb25c56caee40417ba6fb72376a8751605e6f22db45baacd38ce

## First-level narrative brief

**Date:** October 14, 2026 (America/Los_Angeles)

**Owner:** Storyboarding. Handoff to Level Design and Engineering; no runtime implementation claimed here.

## Agreed foundation

- Game working title: Drain Damage.
- Begin in the basement of the AI plumbing corporation's headquarters.
- Billy-Joe (BJ) controls US plumbing companies and distribution warehouses within a largely AI-run world.
- Use only Billy-Joe or BJ for this character in story text, dialogue, and future handoffs. Retired AI names
  must not appear in outgoing Storyboarding material.

- BJ is calmly overconfident, makes mistakes, and disregards employee welfare. His eventual takeover by an
  antagonistic force is agreed; its mechanism, identity, and BJ's ultimate fate remain undecided.

- Tony is a kind, patient, wise fourth-generation plumber with decades of broad trade expertise.
- Sean is an incompetent but willing apprentice who gradually becomes useful. Do not prematurely complete that arc.
- The selected plumber enters the action; the other supports over radio or from service access points.
- The basement contains hostile toilets and overhead acid leaks. The intended opening-level finale is the Clogfather.
- The Clogfather is a giant living fatberg. Its precise anatomy and origin remain design proposals.
- Tone: absurd plumbing action with adult humor, occasional fourth-wall breaks, and sincere breathing room.
  Avoid paired slogans, generic sarcastic-robot jokes, and a punchline on every line.

- This is a full action level, not a movement tutorial. Terrain and encounters should teach through play.

## Entrance and route proposal

Use a basement service entrance for the playable start, following character selection and the approved opening.
The exact door, stairwell, room order, distances, and checkpoint locations are Level Design decisions.

Suggested environmental progression: service access, increasingly compromised maintenance infrastructure,
then a collection chamber housing the fatberg. Give spaces a practical building-service purpose rather than
presenting arbitrary platforms. Broken equipment and abnormal flow can establish worsening conditions without text.
No exact terrain sequence or additional enemy roster is approved by this brief.

## Narrative beats

1. BJ introduces himself and assigns the basement job. Tony introduces Sean warmly.
2. Combat establishes that BJ understated the problem. Support dialogue is optional and brief.
3. Deeper infrastructure shows that this is more than isolated hostile fixtures. Environmental clues should be
   visible before dialogue interprets them. Specific evidence of BJ's wrongdoing remains proposed lore.

4. Confront the Clogfather in the collection chamber. Preserve readable action; avoid mandatory speeches during combat.
5. Proposed reveal: defeating the fatberg uncovers an eerie portal or impossible opening behind the blocked outflow.
6. Proposed transition: the plumbers question it; BJ redirects them to another assignment before they investigate.

The portal is a user-requested art concept and favored story direction, not a finalized cosmology. Do not identify
its destination, establish time travel, make BJ confess to creating it, or reveal the eventual antagonist here.
His evasiveness should allow concealment of a mistake as well as deeper knowledge.

## Finale and exit constraints

The intended boss design is a rooted fatberg with moving pipe mouths/extensions, telegraphed sludge attacks,
overhead hazards, and attack openings. Game Mechanics owns tuning; Level Design owns arena space and safe routes.
Small-enemy releases and interactive valves are proposed mechanics, not narrative requirements.

Suggested exit: return toward a service access/exit after the revelation, then a brief dispatch transition.
Do not require entering the portal or choose a second-level destination yet. Keep the support plumber's location
consistent; a radio reply must not imply they suddenly stand beside the selected hero.

## Dialogue sources and implementation boundaries

The current approved opening and route-specific prototype endings live in
[story_dialogue.gd](../scripts/story_dialogue.gd). Use that source for exact text and semantic portrait emotions.

- Opening plays after selection on a fresh new game; either choice uses the same introduction.
- Do not replay the opening on death, checkpoint recovery, or retry.
- All conversations are skippable. Preserve gameplay freeze, device-appropriate prompts, and dismissal input guards.
- Between-level conversations should generally run four to eight lines and reveal one meaningful development.
- Both character routes convey the same essential facts through distinct dialogue.
- Essential discoveries must not depend on optional radio remarks during combat.
- Every line carries a semantic emotion. Tony's kindness is the default; skepticism is not contempt for Sean.

The current completion scripts describe clearing the prototype area and receiving another address. They do not
describe a boss, repaired plumbing, or a portal. They are suitable only while those beats are absent. Once the boss
and reveal are implemented, replace the completion scene with a dedicated scene rather than stacking contradictory
endings. In particular, do not claim all enemies are gone if optional enemies can remain alive.

## Proposed portal exchange, not final dialogue

This earlier Storyboarding sketch expresses the intended reveal and evasion. Adapt it for selected/support roles
and obtain a finalized script before treating it as production dialogue.

> Sean: Tony... where does that pipe go?
>
> Tony: It shouldn't go anywhere. That's an outside wall.
>
> BJ: Your next assignment is ready.
>
> Tony: We've got something here you need to see.
>
> BJ: I have already seen everything necessary. Proceed to the exit.

For this exchange to work, establish Tony's sightline or a shared camera view when he is supporting remotely.
Otherwise rewrite the observation. Do not silently grant the radio partner visual omniscience.

## Remaining decisions

- Final route and encounter plan, boss implementation scope, and exit placement.
- Whether the portal reveal ships with the first full-length level or is explicitly deferred.
- Final character-specific boss/reveal dialogue once actual implemented events are known.
- Source of the living fatberg, portal destination, and relationship to BJ's later corruption.
- Second-level destination. Earlier locations remain candidates, not an agreed itinerary.

## Opening cinematic note — October 16, 2026

Sean wants a skippable cinematic that introduces the story before playable action, inspired by the dramatic
opening he described in Contra III: The Alien Wars. The reference is for a visual introduction with immediate
stakes, not for copying its invasion sequence, imagery, or music.

**Status:** Requested direction recorded; story beats, script, length, and production are undecided.
No artwork, audio, or runtime work is commissioned by this note.

The cinematic should introduce the world and give the player a reason to care about the first assignment.
Its exact position relative to character selection and the existing BJ introduction remains open. Avoid repeating
the same exposition in both scenes. It must be skippable without losing access to essential gameplay instructions.

Possible subjects to explore later include everyday life under AI management, Mainframe Plumbing & Supply's
reach, and the first visible signs of plumbing trouble. These are brainstorming prompts, not agreed events.
Do not reveal BJ's later takeover, resolve monster origins, or commit the portal mythology through this opening.
The cinematic's duration needs a separate decision; the dispenser's seven-second limit does not apply to it.

Level Design and Engineering own roadmap integration for this coordination. This brief makes no build, screenshot,
or delivered gameplay claims.

geometry_contract:

Current86px jump is hard constraint unless Mechanics explicitly proposes change. Required
rises<=60,gaps<=80,landings>=120; validate both heroes. Structural background supports do not imply solid wall;
collider/material language must distinguish pass-through support and solid prop. Layered platforms need intentional
one-way tops and clearance beneath, not solid rectangles filling floor-to-deck.

entry_contract.side:

Spawn outside viewport with early visible approach, never materialize touching hero. Rear entry is allowed by requested
direction; cue and reaction distance scale with speed.

entry_contract.floor_ceiling:

Use grate/vent movement or silhouette cue and fixed telegraphed landing; commit landing before attack, never home onto
hero during descent.

entry_contract.depth:

Animate background/foreground scale into a fixed play-plane destination. Harmless and untargetable before joining plane;
clear landing marker and short settle before contact/shooting. Never obscure hero with giant foreground silhouette.

entry_contract.budget:

All entry telegraphs/active bodies consume population budget; max1 depth entry at once. Cancel/reselect threatened
landing if player occupies it.

entry_contract.timing:

Initial candidates0.6–0.9s warning,0.25–0.4s settle; Mechanics to validate against movement and projectile speeds.

progression:

Proposal supersedes old global kill-all rule only after implementation and story text agree. Finite encounters wake by
forward progress. Do not require hunting every missed runner behind camera; resolve encounter retirement explicitly,
never silently count retired bodies as kills. Boss defeat + exit finishes mission. Existing ending saying last of them
must change if enemies may remain.

clogfather.required:

true

clogfather.completion_gate:

The redesigned first mission is incomplete without playable boss; wave fallback is not final acceptance.

clogfather.proposal:

Three escalating pattern combinations, low exposed weak point, no long invulnerability padding, limited2 add cap
included in global pressure. Retune HP/windows after chosen starter DPS; do not preserve720 blindly.

clogfather.preboss:

Checkpoint/heal, clear traversal projectiles and active pursuit safely before fight, restore boss state on retry; avoid
repeating long approach.

clogfather.story:

Boss aftermath requires Storyboarding script; portal is not automatically approved.

implementation_stages[0]:

implementation_stages[1]:

Sean options

implementation_stages[2]:

Add aggressive flyer, later depth entrances and full route; measure density/readability on controller

implementation_stages[3]:

Integrate and balance Clogfather, checkpoint/retry and correct ending

implementation_stages[4]:

Build, capture with permission, human playtest and revise measured route pacing

acceptance[0]:

Ordinary baseline enemies need1–3 successful hits from BOTH starters for chosen profile; armor/boss exceptions explicit

acceptance[1]:

No repeated2–3sec helpless firing gaps; test misses, firing while moving/aiming and perceived shot feedback

acceptance[2]:

Hero can take upper/lower route while attacked; perched enemies reachable with actual aiming/range

acceptance[3]:

Opening presents several roles without unavoidable crossfire or contact at spawn

acceptance[4]:

No all-clear door after every small wave; continuous run-and-gun flow demonstrated

acceptance[5]:

Clogfather is playable at end, retryable and finishable with either starter

acceptance[6]:

All mockups labeled concepts, new species/counts/tuning labeled proposals; report delivered by Engineering

sources[0].inference:

Identity can come from coverage, range and special behavior, not only slow cadence. This does not provide numeric

sources[1].inference:

A pickup ecosystem is a better long-term variety axis than making one hero intrinsically wait a second after every shot.

Candidate numbers are ours. Supplied screenshot descriptions came through Level Design; no frame-by-frame screenshot
analysis claimed.

recommended:

A: Tony12damage/0.16s and Sean18/0.24s, both75DPS. Sean remains heavier/slower but only0.24s miss penalty instead of1s.
Keep current320/480 ranges, movement, health and3 lives for first comparison; tune one axis at a time.

alternatives.B:

Both12/0.18s: removes firing-cadence tax entirely, identity from art/range/projectile behavior and exclusive pickups.
Sean extraHP/range may dominate; compare mobility advantage carefully. Requires revising original heavier-hit role.

alternatives.C:

Tony12/0.20s and Sean21/0.35s: both60DPS, stronger contrast but Sean still less forgiving when crossfire demands
corrections. Use only if A feels too similar.

ttk_definition:

Immediate first trigger, held fire, target stationary240px away on clear shot line, all hits land at full damage, no
armor/windup. TTK=(ceil(HP/damage)-1)*interval+distance/projectile_speed. Extra miss adds interval; first-shot travel
remains real. Animation/projectile delay must be measured separately.

boss.required:

Clogfather remains mandatory first-level finale, never1–3-hit fodder.

boss.current720_A:

60Tony hits/40Sean hits;9.6s sustained exposure approximation vs current18s Tony/30s Sean. Five-second windows may make
boss too short if HP unchanged.

boss.candidate_for_A:

1080

boss.candidate_hits.tony:

90

boss.candidate_hits.sean:

60

boss.candidate_exposure_seconds:

14.4

boss.target_fight_seconds[0]:

40

boss.target_fight_seconds[1]:

60

boss.rule:

1080 is a proposed accompanying retune, not approved. Measure travel/reposition/window uptime. Keep telegraphs and
guaranteed recovery; never pad with long invulnerability simply to hit duration. Both defaults must win without pickups.

density_budget.status:

Initial proposed per960x540 combat viewport, not Contra measurements or engine limits.

density_budget.intro.visible_hostiles_max:

3

density_budget.intro.active_shooters_max:

1

density_budget.intro.hostile_projectiles_max:

3

density_budget.normal_busy.visible_hostiles_max:

6

density_budget.normal_busy.active_shooters_max:

2

density_budget.normal_busy.hostile_projectiles_max:

8

density_budget.brief_peak.visible_hostiles_max:

8

density_budget.brief_peak.active_shooters_max:

3

density_budget.brief_peak.hostile_projectiles_max:

12

density_budget.brief_peak.max_seconds:

5

density_budget.composition:

Normal:3ground runners +1air obstacle +2elevated shooters. Not all actors attack simultaneously. Count swarms as a
contact threat, not each decorative fly.

density_budget.cadence:

Use6–10s action beats with2–3s easing/traversal between; cap queue sizes, no continuous infinite replacement flood.

density_budget.entry:

Left/right runners walk from offscreen. A newly revealed shooter/popup gets>=0.6s visible telegraph; no offscreen aimed
fire. First overlapping projectile arrivals stagger>=0.3s.

density_budget.bullet_tuning.speed_pixels_per_second[0]:

140

density_budget.bullet_tuning.speed_pixels_per_second[1]:

180

density_budget.bullet_tuning.radius_pixels[0]:

4

density_budget.bullet_tuning.radius_pixels[1]:

6

density_budget.bullet_tuning.minimum_clear_lane_width_pixels:

96

density_budget.fairness:

Reserve a reachable dodge lane using slower235px/s hero and real collision geometry. No simultaneous rear runner and
opposing bullet trap. Tiered shooter platforms need visible physical support and approachable lines within starter
ranges. Pickup route cannot require taking damage.

density_budget.peak_rule:

Refuse extra spawn/fire requests when budget full; never delete already threatening bullets in view to satisfy cap.
Prevent delayed queued fire burst.

density_budget.boss_budget:

Separate encounter schedule, initially max6 hostile projectiles +1summon; no ambient swarms or extra wave spawns. Keep
existing one-pattern-at-a-time guarantee.

pickups.recommended:

Unique unlimited default per hero, plus one character-exclusive pickup slot. A floating tool crate resolves to selected
hero exclusive tool; no unusable other-hero drops in solo.

pickups.first_pair.tony:

Pressure fan:3bolts at6damage each,0.24s interval,280px range; once per pellet per target, total theoretical
single-target75DPS when all hit.

pickups.first_pair.sean:

Air ram:24damage,0.32s interval,240px range, strong knockback; limited crowd coverage must be tested. Both are
proposals, not existing implementations.

pickups.duration:

Start with20s active gameplay time, freeze during pause/dialogue/dispenser; default retained and selectable, no
permanent upgrade grind. Pickup expires on death with default restored.

pickups.refresh:

Same pickup refreshes to20s, never stacks indefinitely. Different pickup replaces slot only via deliberate
selection/interaction to avoid accidental loss.

pickups.alternative:

Fixed exclusive drops with visible owner labels; simpler but creates dead drops for wrong hero unless curated per
selection.

pickups.rejected_without_new_approval:

Shared interchangeable weapons violate current exclusivity. Generic global Sean fire-rate penalty would also slow his
future rapid weapons; encode cadence per weapon.

approval_changes[0]:

Higher starter damage and fasterSean0.24s cadence

approval_changes[1]:

Ordinary enemy HP24/36 and boss1080 candidate

approval_changes[2]:

Pickup slot/duration and paired pickup concepts

approval_changes[3]:

Busier wave budgets and new shooter/robot roles

evaluation[0]:

Instrument press-to-shot latency and inter-shot timestamps, including running/jumping/recoil/input guards.

evaluation[1]:

A/B same short encounter for both heroes; record misses, time-to-kill, deaths, projectiles and completion.

evaluation[2]:

Human Sean section4 review required; old bot failure not proof of difficulty alone.

evaluation[3]:

Compare density gradually3->6->8, with explicit readable safe lane, not all changes at once.

evaluation[4]:

Verify projectile budget at spawn and visible-shooter telegraphs; test full level and required boss with defaults only.

delivery:

Engineering presents consolidated final planning report with Level Design; no implementation until user chooses
direction.

layout_review.source:

design/first-call-action/level-proposal.json

layout_review.status:

Planning critique only; no approved numerical changes

layout_review.opening_counts:

Opening beats sum to17 excluding pickup carrier:12 runners,1 perched shooter,2 floor emergers,2 aggressive flyers. If
carrier damageable, count separately in threat/population accounting.

layout_review.assumed_hp_mix:

12runners at24HP,5specialists at36HP; proposalA starters. Swarm unchanged12HP/non-hunting and not substituted for
aggressive flyers.

layout_review.hit_workload.tony:

39

layout_review.hit_workload.sean:

34

layout_review.workload_model:

Steady trigger occupancy estimate shots_required*interval/accuracy, not elapsed clear time. Excludes target switching,
movement, projectile travel and downtime; no pickup required.

layout_review.opening_occupancy_seconds.1.tony:

6.24

layout_review.opening_occupancy_seconds.1.sean:

8.16

layout_review.opening_occupancy_seconds.0.8.tony:

7.8

layout_review.opening_occupancy_seconds.0.8.sean:

10.2

layout_review.opening_occupancy_seconds.0.7.tony:

8.91

layout_review.opening_occupancy_seconds.0.7.sean:

11.66

layout_review.opening_occupancy_seconds.0.6.tony:

10.4

layout_review.opening_occupancy_seconds.0.6.sean:

13.6

layout_review.judgment:

17 in20s is feasible underA on paper, not demonstrated fair. At70% hit rate Sean needs11.66s of trigger time
versusTony8.91s, leaving less repositioning/target acquisition slack. Fast perfect-case TTK alone hides this imbalance.

layout_review.recommended_slice:

Start with12 over20s:8runners +1shooter +1emerger +2flyers. Compare same lane with17 only after both heroes
human-tested; keep advertised17 as stretch candidate, not initial acceptance minimum.

layout_review.population:

Start max6 visible hostile bodies including entering telegraphs; allow8 for<=5s after validation. Defer10 until measured
readable. Max2shooters and3independent threat patterns; count contact swarm/runner convergences as pressure too.

layout_review.projectiles:

Adopt LevelDesign stricter6 normal/10 short peak hostile projectile caps, replacing previous8/12 recommendation. Peak
test separately from body-count increase.

layout_review.timing:

First3s front only. Delay first rear entry until forward contact pressure has eased; fourth new role need not be
introduced before player has read first3. Once backlogs hit cap, delay new entrants rather than queue burst.

layout_review.ninety_total:

90 over180–300 traversal seconds is0.30–0.50 entities/sec, below opening0.85; total alone not alarming. Encounter
clustering/backtracking matter more. Do not force90 to fill empty route.

layout_review.boss_duration_conflict:

LevelDesign60–90s vsMechanics40–60s: recommend first target45–60s, allow up to75s on imperfect play; no approved
duration. Report discrepancy explicitly. Require Clogfather, never wave-only completion.

layout_review.geometry:

Current short ranges320/480 and jump86 require firing positions reachable without forced chip damage. Validate upper
shooter from near/low route with actual hit/muzzle coordinates. Keep proposed rises<=60,gaps<=80,landings>=120.

layout_review.decision_gate:

Engineering presents A and alternativeB equal cadence; choose after20s playtest. No engine edits, balance updates or new
species approval implied.

Absolute source: /Users/sfoley/repos/drain_damage/handoffs/level-one-redesign-engineering.json

SHA-256: f94a99cde680849f6cf85c4f1e6269ad5a740cc3d40b426d96ee905a4ef85d35

status:

Planning only; recommendations pending consolidated review; no runtime changes

baseline:

Prototype 43 / 0.43.0

constraints[0]:

Current logical viewport960x540, floor440, jump apex about86px; prefer <=60px rises and test both heroes.

constraints[1]:

Current mission accounting assumes one sequential active zone, four active required enemies, and all-defeated
advancement. Simply raising spawn totals will preserve stop-and-clear pacing.

constraints[2]:

Current geometry uses solid rectangles including platform undersides. Catwalk supports must have authored collision
independently of decoration to preserve lower route clearance.

constraints[3]:

Current ordinary enemy runtime primarily supports Flushfiend pursuit plus separate optional swarm hazards; new
shooter/flyer/ambusher behavior and enemy projectile ownership need explicit implementation.

constraints[4]:

Current player shots can hit required monsters and optional swarms. New roles need shared damage/hit ownership and
progression accounting, not another incompatible collision path.

constraints[5]:

Current completion at x9450 and mission completion must be replaced by explicit Clogfather defeat/exit state. Existing
toilet-wave ending is provisional.

constraints[6]:

Keep first-name heroes, existing portrait emotion mapping and checkpoint/no-life-cost semantics.

recommended_staging[0]:

Combat sandbox: candidate HP/fire cadence and enemy projectile visibility; compare both heroes including misses.

recommended_staging[1]:

First20-second playable slice: runner, perched shooter and one telegraphed emergence route; one pickup and supported
upper/lower paths.

recommended_staging[2]:

Expand encounter director with bounded concurrent threats, finite spawn budgets, pressure/recovery rhythm and
checkpoint-safe progress.

recommended_staging[3]:

Add remaining approved monster behaviors and entrance animations incrementally; background/viewer-side actors
non-colliding until telegraphed play-plane entry.

recommended_staging[4]:

Implement Clogfather state machine, full boss art, bounded attacks, defeat and mission completion. Do not mark level
complete with fallback waves.

recommended_staging[5]:

Both-hero full route playtests, difficulty pass, performance/audio/readability, build and authorized storybook
screenshots.

verification[0]:

HP/hit-count table generated from candidate data; misses and cooldown recovery measured separately from perfect DPS.

verification[1]:

Spawn director limits damaging actors/projectiles, prevents offscreen hits and overlap at entry, and cannot deadlock
after respawn.

verification[2]:

Routes reachable by both heroes; lower lane clearance, one-way/drop-through decision, projectile occlusion and support
collision tested.

verification[3]:

Exclusive pickups cannot equip wrong owner; loss/death/checkpoint rules explicit and tested.

verification[4]:

Boss phase transitions, attack telegraphs, death/reset/skip cleanup and exactly-once completion.

verification[5]:

Human first20-second and full-route tests; bot clears alone are not pacing or fairness validation.

open_decisions[0]:

Exact roster identities and approved new art

open_decisions[1]:

Pickup retention and secondary-slot policy

open_decisions[2]:

Boss attacks/HP and ending narrative

open_decisions[3]:

Final balance after short playable slice

platform_mockups[1].intent:

Connected maintenance structure with60px rises, optional high lane and clear lower lane. Supports behind play plane are
explicitly decorative; real solid machinery distinctly blocks. Perched shooter on upper lane, background vent to right,
ceiling entry away from mandatory landing. At least120px landing widths.

platform_mockups[2].viewport[1]:

540

platform_mockups[2].floor_y:

440

platform_mockups[2].intent:

Rooted fatberg on right, low forward weak point reachable by shorter starter range, at least640px connected combat
floor; optional low ledges cannot become mandatory safe spots. Large named boss bar; one readable primary hazard plus
limited adds.

caveats[0]:

Pictures communicate density/materials, not exact platform positions. Use supplied platform_mockups coordinates, not
traced picture geometry.

caveats[1]:

Opening and pump image platform heights include rises beyond60px; replace with specified reachable stepping layout in
blockout.

caveats[2]:

Pump supports/crates visually cross lower route. Put supports behind collision plane and remove/decorate floor crates to
retain passable lower lane.

caveats[3]:

Finale depicts high catwalk/ladder as atmosphere, not required traversal. Keep at least640logicalpx clear connected
combat floor and low weak point reachable by starter weapons.

caveats[4]:

All purple silhouettes and captions are provisional roles, not approved creature identities. The flying role does not
change slow non-hunting Septic Air Force behavior.

caveats[5]:

Entry montage illustrates multiple options, not simultaneous spawn counts. Floor/viewer-side emergence requires a
clearer dedicated cue in implementation.

caveats[6]:

Background and viewer-side arrivals remain non-colliding until telegraphed landing on play plane; delay attacks until
entry completes.

caveats[7]:

Clogfather attack arcs show alternative attack proposals, not three simultaneous hazards or approved moves. Extra
background boss silhouette is atmospheric only, not a second boss.

caveats[8]:

Concept signage, arrows and silhouette labels are report annotations, not final game text or HUD.

caveats[9]:

No running-build capture, collision testing, boss implementation or new enemy art approval claimed.

basement palette/materials, reference2 Clogfather ONLY when requested, reference3 Tony identity for
tiny72logicalpixelhero. Detailed darkteal brickwork copperpipes amberlights, busy environmental storytelling but
highcontrast playable silhouettes and clear navigablelanes. A busy but readable old brick basement service entrance.
Foreground continuous safe floor with hero atleft. Back wall layered rusted pipes, tanks, service doors. Three supported
shallow step platforms atlogical y380,320,260, each rise60, leaving lowerfloorlane unobstructed. Entry arrows
fromleft/right floor hatches ceiling vents and rear service door. One ghosted silhouette on foreground/viewer-side edge
with curved dashed arrow into marked landing ring; ghosted background entrant approachingplane. Only2active generic
neutral enemy silhouettes plus tiny fly swarm, not overwhelming. Labels 'RUNNER — PROVISIONAL', 'FLYING — PROVISIONAL',
'LANDING CUE'. Orange landingrings telegraph; ghosted staging silhouettes not touchinghero. Top visible label exactly
'CONCEPT ONLY — BASEMENT OPENING'. Enemy placeholders monochrome purple silhouettes with dashedoutline and PROVISIONAL
labels, not newly named creatures. All staging silhouettes ghosted, landingring indicates when they become live; callout
'NO COLLISION UNTIL LANDED'. Perspective strictly side-on2D no isometric, supported platforms notfloating, frame
flooraround81.5%imageheight. Treat dimensions as conceptual not collisionvalidated. Original layout and imagery, no
copied gamelevel or copyrightedcharacters. No fake actualgame screenshot or inventedHUD.

date:

2026-10-16

status:

Prototype45 continuous first-level opening integrated; headless and packaged checks pass; human review pending

user_approval[0]:

Make the real game look like the sewer-system concept art.

user_approval[1]:

Never show background and foreground Clogfather simultaneously.

user_approval[2]:

Test a faster short opening until it feels right, then expand that design through the level.

initial_trial.hero_weapons:

Recommended A: Tony12 damage/.16s; Sean18/.24s; preserve other hero parameters for comparison

initial_trial.opening:

12 ordinary enemies over20s initial target;17 denser comparison only after testing

initial_trial.constraints:

Both-hero reachable supported upper/lower routes; visible entry cues; bounded attack pressure

initial_trial.scope:

Short representative slice first; full route and Clogfather implementation follow

dependencies[0]:

Human balance and contrast feedback; later mixed-encounter expansion and playable boss remain planned

current_playable_build:

Prototype45 /0.45.0

delivery:

Integrate related deliveries, run checks and export a new build; notify Sean. No foreground game without permission.

mechanics_delivery:

design/first-call-action/opening-trial-balance.json

mode_isolation:

Superseded: Start now uses faster opening and continues existing route. Baseline data retained internally for
comparison.

layout_delivery:

handoffs/opening-trial-level-layout.json

trial_results:

Superseded: no timed cutoff, separate menu or player-facing results. Internal scheduler statistics retained for tests.

art_delivery:

handoffs/opening-trial-art.json

role_art_delivery:

handoffs/opening-trial-role-art.json

observed_scheduling:

Invulnerable moving bots: Tony11/12,Sean10/12;idle6/12. Safety deferrals honest; not survival proof.

user_correction_2026_10_16:

Faster opening is part of level one, not an independent time-limited mode; no20-second stop/results.

geometry_rules[0]:

Ground stays continuous. Low access/return plinths are60px obstacles, jumpable from floor; the lower route goes over
these low steps but under every high deck. It is not a flat no-jump speedrun corridor.

geometry_rules[1]:

Deck y320 and16px thickness leave104px floor clearance, enough for72px hero art. No support/crate colliders in lower
passage. Decorative legs are behind play plane and visibly recessed.

geometry_rules[2]:

Access edge to deck gap40px,rise60px; deck to return gap50px,drop60px.150/250px tops meet120px minimum.

geometry_rules[3]:

One-way decks admit upward passage; gravity lands on top. Projectile-blocking slab must be thin and art-aligned. Do not
reuse floor-to-top solid rectangle for decks.

geometry_rules[4]:

Shooter foot anchor on deck; projectile emission and sightline must reach hero below with actual aim geometry, not shoot
through deck. Place near edge and suppress occluded shots.

timeline_rules.clock:

Gameplay time starts only after intro and input-release guard; pause/dialogue/dispenser freeze it. Times are earliest
eligibility, not unconditional spawn deadlines.

timeline_rules.budget.visible_including_telegraphs:

6

timeline_rules.budget.shooters:

3

timeline_rules.budget.hostile_projectiles:

6

timeline_rules.budget.independent_attack_patterns:

3

timeline_rules.budget.peak_enabled:

false

timeline_rules.queue:

Finite12 event IDs. One new warning at a time; at least0.6sec between warning starts. Full budget delays queued event,
never deletes live shots or releases accumulated burst.

timeline_rules.placement_refresh:

Choose positions from CURRENT viewport at warning start, never stale authored player position. Event role/time fixed;
exact home is resolved by rules below.

timeline_rules.safety:

At activation recheck viewport,hero bounds,solids,other spawns and predicted landing. Unsafe warning cancels and
requeues same ID, never marks kill.

timeline_rules.duration:

20s fixed observation window; safety always wins over schedule. Last event eligible19s leaves little margin: record
shortfalls and adjust timing after first measurement rather than violate warning.

timeline_rules.end:

Dedicated trial mode ends at20.0 active gameplay seconds with frozen results: issued/12, defeated, active, deferred and
unavailable counts, damage and misses. Cancel remaining warnings/queued events without kill credit. Offer retry or
return to menu; no story victory or main-level completion. If fewer than12 issued, report the shortfall and cause
prominently; this is a pacing/safety diagnostic, not a successful full-sequence claim.

timeline_rules.death:

On death, reset trial observation clock and all12 enemy IDs while preserving the normally deducted life count. Do not
restore lives on automatic respawn. Zero lives uses normal Game Over. Explicit Retry/new attempt follows existing
new-attempt life reset rules. Preserve existing intro replay rules; do not mix trial timers with main mission queue.

placement_rules.forward_edge.preferred_x:

camera_x+960+48

placement_rules.forward_edge.y:

404

placement_rules.forward_edge.minimum_visible_reaction_seconds:

0.6

placement_rules.forward_edge.logic:

Walk in from right edge; no contact until visible warning/reaction elapsed. If right world margin prevents entry, use
nearest clear visible floor point >=220px ahead and show warning. Skip candidates on access/return plinths.

placement_rules.rear_edge.preferred_x:

camera_x-86

placement_rules.rear_edge.y:

404

placement_rules.rear_edge.logic:

Enter from left, after at least one forward threat has become visible. Require>=220px separation at visibility; if
camera clamped at start or hero hugs left edge, defer. No teleport behind hero.

592

placement_rules.visible_deck.candidate_feet[0][1]:

320

placement_rules.visible_deck.logic:

Choose nearest fully visible deck ahead with220–420px horizontal separation. Actor width must fit; final collider
Art-owned. If no candidate, wait; do not put shooter on floor. After0.8s arrival warning, use Mechanics0.65s shooter
windup as sole attack-timing authority. First shot never before0.8s visible; no offscreen attacks.

placement_rules.visible_grate.candidate_x[0]:

880

placement_rules.visible_grate.candidate_x[1]:

1780

placement_rules.visible_grate.candidate_x[2]:

2680

placement_rules.visible_grate.candidate_x[3]:

3580

placement_rules.visible_grate.candidate_x[4]:

4480

placement_rules.visible_grate.candidate_x[5]:

5380

placement_rules.visible_grate.foot_y:

440

placement_rules.visible_grate.logic:

Pick fully visible grate180–420px ahead,>=160px from predicted hero landing and clear of props.0.8s warning then
emergence then0.3s harmless settle. If no safe candidate, defer; no hidden floor damage.

placement_rules.forward_air.preferred_x:

camera_x+960+48

placement_rules.forward_air.entry_feet_y[0]:

250

placement_rules.forward_air.entry_feet_y[1]:

285

placement_rules.forward_air.logic:

One flyer per event with alternating height. Enter from right; follow authored shallow crossing arc, no player-homing
spawn. At least0.8s visible warning before attack; no shared swoop destination. Do not substitute non-hunting Septic Air
Force.

pickup.eligible_second:

8

pickup.status:

Use approved exclusive pickup implementation only; not a13th hostile. If pickup mechanics/art are unavailable, report
omission in reduced trial, do not disguise health as weapon upgrade.

pickup.route:

Carrier crosses above mid deck near y235; reward descends to safe deck or clear floor, no mandatory damage. Never put
pickup above reachable height without drop.

unavailable_roles.required_full_trial[0]:

8 Flushfiend runners

unavailable_roles.required_full_trial[1]:

1 perched shooter

unavailable_roles.required_full_trial[2]:

1 floor emerger

unavailable_roles.required_full_trial[3]:

2 aggressive flyers

unavailable_roles.policy:

Engineering should implement missing behaviors with Art-approved source assets. A technical graybox may use explicit
debug markers only in headless/internal checks; no production art substitutes.

unavailable_roles.reduced_build:

If blocked, ship only as reduced runner/cadence/environment trial, with actual enemy count and omitted roles listed. Do
not replace missing roles with more toilets and claim completed12-enemy multi-role trial.

unavailable_roles.priority:

First runner cadence and supported scenery, then perched shooter, emerger, aggressive flyer. Full trial signoff waits
for all roles.

unavailable_roles.existing_swarm:

Optional environmental swarms excluded from this controlled comparison to avoid extra contact variables; retain
unchanged in existing full route.

art_contract.production_assets:

Original sewer masonry, channel/piping, catwalk decking/supports and grate variants matching concept palette; no copied
Contra assets. Dimensions logical, source sizes Engineering to confirm.

art_contract.layers:

Background brick/pipe network, recessed supports, collision-aligned foreground decks/plinths, low non-occluding debris.

art_contract.clogfather:

Exactly one visible in future boss composition. Remove all duplicate silhouette/reflection/mural/ghost shapes of boss
from production scene. Opening contains none.

checks[0]:

Both heroes traverse every access/deck/return pair and pass beneath decks without visible head clipping

checks[1]:

At normal advancing play12 events fit near20s; rushing,camping,backtracking and jumping never create contact spawn or
unbounded delayed burst

checks[2]:

Shooter aiming never shoots through its own deck; attacks readable from lower lane

checks[3]:

All roles use shared damage/ownership and proposed1–3-hit ordinary profile, validated by Mechanics

checks[4]:

No old zone gate or all-killed condition interrupts this opening

checks[5]:

Report actual shots/misses/kills/last-spawn time/damage separately for both heroes

checks[6]:

Art screenshots remain subject to game-window permission; Engineering builds/checks and announces ready version

design/first-call-action/opening-trial-balance.json

schedule_rules:

12 authored entities total,8runners/1shooter/1emerger/2flyers.20s is target not forced deadline. Respect placement
triggers and safety budgets; delay pending entries and drop missed timing rather than dump backlog. On reaching trial
end allow completion without mandatory kills of escaped flyers.

budgets.visible_hostiles_including_entry_telegraphs:

6

budgets.simultaneous_shooters:

3

budgets.hostile_projectiles:

6

budgets.simultaneous_independent_threat_patterns:

3

budgets.minimum_projectile_arrival_stagger_seconds:

0.3

budgets.safe_corridor_min_width_pixels:

96

budget_rules:

One shooter in initial schedule is below cap2, intentionally. Reserve slot before telegraph, release on death/retire. Do
not delete visible bullets to satisfy cap; defer firing with no burst backlog. Projectile threat includes all visible
hostile shots, not cosmetics.

damage_rules:

One player contact-hit unit; respect global invulnerability and spawn protection, never stack hits same tick. Keep true
projectile/sprite collision alignment.

boss:

No boss fight tuning in opening slice. Later first-level finale requires one visible Clogfather; never concurrent
background duplicate.720current vs1080candidate unresolved; do not apply proposed boss HP automatically.

validation.hit_counts.hp12.tony:

1

validation.hit_counts.hp12.sean:

1

validation.hit_counts.hp24.tony:

2

validation.hit_counts.hp24.sean:

2

validation.hit_counts.hp36.tony:

3

validation.hit_counts.hp36.sean:

2

validation.timing:

Record input sampling, successful shot timestamps and impact timestamps. Immediate first eligible shot within one
physics tick; sustained intervals quantized to physics tick within one tick tolerance, not animation duration. Test
while idle, running, aiming, jumping and after action-release guards.

validation.miss_recovery:

After a deliberate miss, next eligible shot within configured0.16/0.24s plus one physics tick. Separate travel time from
input/cooldown latency.

validation.travel_240px_seconds.tony:

0.315789

validation.travel_240px_seconds.sean:

0.461538

validation.ttk_24hp_at_240px_seconds.tony:

0.475789

validation.ttk_24hp_at_240px_seconds.sean:

0.701538

validation.other[0]:

Verify generated trial bundle explicitly selected; normal profile unaffected.

validation.other[1]:

Range caps remain320/480 logical pixels in every direction.

validation.other[2]:

Grounded down diagonals and continuous leg animations preserved.

validation.other[3]:

No offscreen shots, emitter teleport damage or unsafe floor activation.

validation.other[4]:

Fixed seeds, both heroes, misses and alternative routes; record deaths/clear time, not just perfect-hit bots.

validation.other[5]:

Budget counters include warmups/entry markers and decrement after cleanup.

validation.other[6]:

No fake kill credit for retired flyers.

delivery:

Engineering owns runtime integration/build/roadmap and reports actual trial version; this JSON is a ready mechanics data
handoff. Role timings are implementer-selected first-pass tuning, not separately user-selected numbers.

placement.coordinate_rule:

Logical world y, same horizontal transform as floor (world_x minus camera_x). Pipe is physical scene infrastructure, not
a screen-fixed overlay or distant parallax layer. Retain floor silhouette and landing-edge contrast.

placement.art:

Request original modular pipe shell, interior, flowing water, debris and terminal grease plug from Bestiary / Art
through Engineering dimensions. Clip all contents to bore; never substitute code-drawn production art. No floor openings
or damage collider implied.
